./claude/reports/2026-08-22/DB_TRUTH_FORK_2026-08-22B.md:281:So canonical Python source has no `~/empire-repo/...` string. But the **`unified_message_store.py` hardcodes the fork path** via `Path("~/empire-repo/...")`:
./claude/reports/2026-08-22/DB_TRUTH_FORK_2026-08-22B.md:289:`unified_message_store.py:16` — **HARDCODED** to `~/empire-repo/backend/data/brain/unified_messages.db`. This is the smoking gun: when the canonical backend runs, its `unified_message_store` writes to the **fork's** brain. That's why the fork `unified_messages.db` is 23.3 MB mtime today while the canonical is 22.1 MB mtime Jun 23.
./claude/reports/2026-08-22/DB_TRUTH_FORK_2026-08-22B.md:292:- The canonical backend (pid 652663) → writes to `empire-data/empire.db` (corridor), `empire-data/empirebox.db` (the empty SQLAlchemy default), and **via `unified_message_store.py` hardcode** to `~/empire-repo/backend/data/brain/unified_messages.db`.
./claude/reports/2026-08-22/DB_TRUTH_FORK_2026-08-22B.md:293:- The canonical OpenClaw worker (pid 1755, runs in canonical tree) → writes the heartbeat to `~/empire-repo/backend/data/max/openclaw_worker_heartbeat.json` (mtime 11:28 today). Source of this write path: hardcoded in OpenClaw's worker code.
./claude/reports/2026-08-22/DB_TRUTH_FORK_2026-08-22B.md:294:- The nightly `brain_sync` (scheduler.py:287, runs 23:00) → writes `~/empire-repo/backend/data/max/memory.md` mtime 8/21 23:00 (last fired last night). Reads from canonical, writes to fork because of a `Path` resolution that defaults to the fork (the resolution order at scheduler.py:300 explicitly tries `MAX_MEMORY_PATH` env → canonical `<repo>/max/memory.md` → legacy fork fallback; the legacy fallback is what fires today because there's no `MAX_MEMORY_PATH` and the canonical repo path is computed but may not exist in the run-time environment).

total reflects caramel-leather material + curved-bench labor + channel-panel labor\n\n### \u26a0\ufe0f Action Needed\n- **Deposit $1,450 not yet received**\n- Flammability compliance confirmation pending\n- No job created yet (job_id is null)\n\n\ud83d\udccd **DC** \u00b7 Status: \ud83d\udfe1 Draft (never sent)\n\ud83c\udd94 Quote ID: `54f4f121` \u00b7 Migrated from legacy JSON\n\n### Project\nOriginal ballroom entrance doors quote with 3 fabric tiers (A/B/C).\n\n### Financials\n| Field | Amount |\n|---|---|\n| Subtotal | $0.00 |\n| Total | **$0.00** |\n| Deposit required | $0.00 |\n| Balance due | $0.00 |\n\n### Status\n- Created: 2026-03-18\n- Last updated: 2026-03-27\n- Never priced, never sent\n- Source: `migrated` from `/home/rg/empire-repo/backend/data/quotes/54f4f121.json`\n- Contains 4 line items across 3 tiers (Essential/Standard/Premium) for ballroom door panels\n\n### \u26a0\ufe0f Note\nThis is a **stale draft from March 2026** \u2014 superseded by EST-2026-110 (the RUSH bench + panel project). Safe to delete or archive.\n\n**Summary:** EST-2026-110 is the live active quote ($2,900, sent, awaiting deposit). ./backend/data/chats/founder/7f5800fd.json:16:      "content": "<think>sqlite3 is not installed. Let me try python3 to query the database instead.\n</think>\n\nLet me use Python to query the database:\n\n[TOOL_CALL]\n{\"tool\": \"shell_execute\", \"command\": \"cd ~/empire-repo && python3 -c \\\"\\nimport sqlite3, json\\nconn = sqlite3.connect('backend/empire.db')\\nconn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\\ncur = conn.cursor()\\ncur.execute(\\\\\\\"SELECT id, source, content, created_at FROM unified_messages WHERE source='web_chat' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 5\\\\\\\")\\nrows = cur.fetchall()\\nfor r in rows:\\n    content = r['content']\\n    try:\\n        obj = json.loads(content)\\n        text = obj.get('text', obj.get('content', ''))\\n    except:\\n        text = content[:200]\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"ID: {r['id']} | Time: {r['created_at']} | Text: {text}\\\\\\\")\\nconn.close()\\n\\\"\"}\n[/TOOL_CALL]",
